## Cybersecurity — OSCP M11 concept-page layer (batch-3, 2026-06-05)

Added 2026-06-05 from the M11 concept-page batch-3 fan-out (heavy stress-test of the open-N-ary substrate-composition unlock). Introduces 7 NEW candidate third-axes (protocol-poisoning · implant-state-machine · lolbin-substrate · secret-discovery-substrate · persistence-mechanism · load-order-hijack · log-suppression) plus reverse-symmetry axis at 4th–5th total stress-instances.

### Persistence + Defense Evasion

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **WmiPrvSE.exe** | WMI Provider Host — parent process of all permanent event consumer payloads. Provides the fileless-persistence reentry vector for wmi-event-subscription-persistence. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §Persistence-mechanism axis |
| **__EventFilter** | WMI class holding the WQL trigger query that fires a permanent event consumer. First call in the S·F·C·B deployment sequence. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §Filter |
| **__FilterToConsumerBinding** | WMI class wiring an __EventFilter to its consumer — IS the launcher. Fourth call in S·F·C·B; this object IS the standing trigger. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §Binding |
| **CommandLineEventConsumer / ActiveScriptEventConsumer / LogFileEventConsumer** | The 3 OSCP-relevant WMI permanent event consumer subclasses (CAL mnemonic). | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §Consumer |
| **root\subscription** | WMI namespace holding permanent event subscription instances. Not root\cimv2 — that's where WQL queries run, not where instances live. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §namespace |
| **Sysmon EID 19/20/21** | WmiEventFilter / WmiEventConsumer / WmiEventConsumerToFilter activity. 1:1 mapping to the F·C·B triplet. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §Defender mirror |
| **Microsoft-Windows-WMI-Activity/Operational EID 5861** | Permanent consumer execution event — fires at each trigger. Defender's runtime evidence of WMI persistence. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence §Defender mirror |
| **DLL Search Order Hijack** | First of 4 dll-sideloading-catalog mechanisms — drop a malicious DLL into the application directory (step 1 of the 8-position Windows DLL search order). | dll-sideloading-catalog §Mechanism 1 |
| **Phantom DLL** | Second of 4 mechanisms — supply a DLL the application requests by name but Windows has never shipped. | dll-sideloading-catalog §Mechanism 2 |
| **Proxy DLL** | Third of 4 mechanisms — replace a legitimate DLL with a malicious shim that forwards exports to the real one (call-through stub). | dll-sideloading-catalog §Mechanism 3 |
| **Manifest Redirection** | Fourth of 4 mechanisms — orthogonal to mechanisms 1-3; rewrites the application manifest to point assembly references at attacker-controlled paths. | dll-sideloading-catalog §Mechanism 4 |
| **WPTSEXTENSIONS** | RTFM §3 canonical Windows 10 DLL hijack recipe targeting `wptsextensions.dll`. Concrete worked example of mechanism 1. | dll-sideloading-catalog §RTFM recipe |
| **KnownDlls registry** | `HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Session Manager\KnownDlls` — the 0th position in the 8-position load order; entries here bypass all 4 hijack mechanisms. | dll-sideloading-catalog §Defender mirror |
| **Sysmon EID 7 ImageLoad** | Module-load monitoring event — defender's primary detection for all 4 DLL hijack mechanisms. | dll-sideloading-catalog §Defender mirror |
| **WDAC** | Windows Defender Application Control — code-integrity policy enforcement layer; canonical mitigation for LOLBin abuse and DLL sideloading. | dll-sideloading-catalog · event-log-evasion-techniques §Defender mirror |

### Persistence-mechanism (substrate axis)

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **persistence-mechanism (substrate axis)** | The third-axis category introduced by wmi-event-subscription-persistence — names autonomic OS-resident infrastructure repurposed as a standing self-firing trigger. Structurally distinct from command catalog (no typed strings), framework (no orchestration), graph-theoretic (no traversal). The FilterToConsumerBinding IS the launcher. | wmi-event-subscription-persistence |
| **load-order-hijack (substrate axis)** | The third-axis category introduced by dll-sideloading-catalog — names the Windows DLL resolution algorithm itself as the substrate; attacker exploits which directory wins the deterministic search-order race. 4 orthogonal intercept points (Search Order / Phantom / Proxy / Manifest Redirection) on the same documented loader algorithm. | dll-sideloading-catalog |
| **log-suppression (substrate axis)** | The third-axis category introduced by event-log-evasion-techniques — names the negation surface of the defender's telemetry plane. 5 classes attack distinct EventLog stack layers (service · .evtx file · userland API prologue · provider registry · circular buffer). | event-log-evasion-techniques |

### Broadcast-resolution poisoning

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **protocol-poisoning (substrate axis)** | The third-axis category introduced by responder-llmnr-poisoning — names broadcast-domain-peer race condition as offensive primitive; first-responder wins, attacker races and lies. Generalizes across LLMNR/NBT-NS/mDNS with port + packet-format substitution. Structurally distinct from tunneling-protocol (rides inside) and protocol-decision-tree (branches across). | responder-llmnr-poisoning |
| **LLMNR** | Link-Local Multicast Name Resolution (UDP 5355, IPv4 224.0.0.252 / IPv6 ff02::1:3) — Windows fallback name resolution; victim broadcasts unresolved hostname, Responder answers. | responder-llmnr-poisoning §Protocol 1 |
| **NBT-NS** | NetBIOS Name Service (UDP 137) — legacy broadcast name resolution; same poison pattern as LLMNR at L2 broadcast TTL. | responder-llmnr-poisoning §Protocol 2 |
| **mDNS** | Multicast DNS (UDP 5353, 224.0.0.251) — Apple Bonjour / zero-conf name resolution; rarely Responder-targeted in Windows-only AD environments but same primitive. | responder-llmnr-poisoning §Protocol 3 |
| **NetNTLMv1 / NetNTLMv2** | Challenge-response hash format captured by Responder — NOT the same as NTLM hash. NetNTLMv2 = HMAC-MD5 over server challenge + client challenge + target info; relay-viable when target SMB signing OFF. | responder-llmnr-poisoning §Hash format |
| **ntlmrelayx** | impacket relay tool — accepts NetNTLM authentication from Responder-captured victim and replays to a second target whose SMB signing is disabled. Yields SYSTEM via SMB authenticated relay. | responder-llmnr-poisoning §Relay mode |

### C2 framework lifecycle

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **implant-state-machine (substrate axis)** | The third-axis category introduced by c2-frameworks-comparison — first **temporal/protocol substrate** in the open-N-ary set. 6 states + 7 transitions parameterized by sleep interval, jitter, check-in protocol, task-queue state. Structurally distinct from spatial taxonomies (graph-theoretic, reverse-symmetry), command catalogs, and tool-construction. | c2-frameworks-comparison |
| **OFTERS** | 6-state implant lifecycle mnemonic: **O**ffline · **F**irst-call · **T**asked · **E**xecuting · **R**eporting · **S**leeping. Universal across the 5 frameworks in c2-frameworks-comparison. | c2-frameworks-comparison §State machine |
| **MEM-SMH** | 5-framework mnemonic for c2-frameworks-comparison: **M**etasploit · **E**mpire · **S**liver · **M**ythic · **H**avoc. | c2-frameworks-comparison §Framework set |
| **TSIBPO** | 6-axis comparison mnemonic for c2-frameworks-comparison (Transport · Sleep-mask · Implant-language · BOF-runtime · Protocol-pluggability · OPSEC-defaults). | c2-frameworks-comparison §Axis set |
| **sleep-mask** | Implant memory-encryption technique applied during sleep cycles — discriminator between Havoc/Sliver (yes) and Meterpreter (no). Defeats memory-scanning EDR during dormancy. | c2-frameworks-comparison §T6 transition |
| **JA3 fingerprint** | TLS client-hello fingerprint hash — load-bearing defender signal for C2 traffic identification across frameworks regardless of destination domain. | c2-frameworks-comparison §Defender mirror |

### Credential discovery (registry hives)

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **secret-discovery-substrate (substrate axis)** | The third-axis category introduced by passwords-in-windows-registry — names *known-leak locations* (paths where OS or third-party software violated its own secure-storage contract) as a discovery primitive. Unit of value is the contract violation, not the operation. Categorically different from command-catalog (active typed strings), framework, or graph-theoretic. | passwords-in-windows-registry |
| **AutoLogon registry path** | `HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Winlogon` (DefaultUserName · DefaultPassword · AutoAdminLogon) — cleartext credential leak; 5-star yield quick-win. | passwords-in-windows-registry §Location 1 |
| **GPP cpassword** | Group Policy Preferences XML stored under SYSVOL with AES-256 password encrypted using the publicly-documented 32-byte AES key (MS published it in MS14-025 KB) — effectively cleartext. | passwords-in-windows-registry §Location 5 |
| **PSReadLine ConsoleHost_history** | `%APPDATA%\Microsoft\Windows\PowerShell\PSReadLine\ConsoleHost_history.txt` — PowerShell command-line history; routinely contains typed credentials, connection strings, API tokens. | passwords-in-windows-registry §Location 7 |
| **DPAPI master key** | Windows Data Protection API per-user master key derived from user password hash — protects PuTTY/Chrome/Edge stored secrets; offline-extractable when SYSTEM-equivalent with user hash. | passwords-in-windows-registry §DPAPI tier |

### LOLBin layer (partial — page reroll pending in batch-4)

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **LOLBin** | Living-Off-The-Land Binary — signed Microsoft binary abused for offensive action. Trust-anchor exploitation: every entry is on every default-allow code-integrity policy. (Owner page pending re-author in batch-4 — page reported complete in batch-3 synthesizer but file absent on disk.) | (pending: living-off-the-land-binaries) |
| **LOLBAS** | Living Off The Land Binaries And Scripts project — canonical registry at lolbas-project.github.io. | (pending: living-off-the-land-binaries) |
| **Squiblydoo** | `regsvr32 /i:http:// scrobj.dll` remote scriptlet execution technique — canonical signed-binary remote-payload-load primitive. | (pending: living-off-the-land-binaries) |
| **lolbin-substrate (substrate axis)** | Candidate third-axis category — names trust-anchor exploitation (binary is already signed by Microsoft, invisible to static-allowlist defense) as substrate distinct from command (active typed catalog) and framework (no orchestration database). Page pending. | (pending: living-off-the-land-binaries) |

### Memory forensics + binary signature engineering

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **PNCRS** | 5-family Volatility plugin taxonomy mnemonic: **P**rocess · **N**etwork · **C**ode-injection · **R**egistry · **S**tring-hash. 12 plugins in 3-2-3-3-3 distribution. | volatility-memory-forensics §Family taxonomy |
| **malfind** | Volatility plugin detecting injected code by scanning VAD entries for RX-protected private-mapped regions with executable headers. Dominant plugin for heap-allocated-malware case. | volatility-memory-forensics §F3 Code-injection |
| **winpmem / DumpIt** | Memory acquisition tools — winpmem (kernel driver, Volatility-friendly) and DumpIt (single-binary, raw .raw). Acquisition prerequisite per RFC 3227 volatility-of-evidence ordering (RAM before disk). | volatility-memory-forensics §Acquisition |
| **EPROCESS** | Windows kernel structure representing a process — Volatility's traversal anchor for psscan / pstree / dlllist. Silicon-axis termination per Erickson §0x200. | volatility-memory-forensics §Silicon substrate |
| **WNSM pipeline** | 4-step Mandiant imphash pipeline mnemonic: **W**alk IDT → **N**ormalize → **S**ort-by-IDT-order → **M**D5. Non-sort trap: step 3 is IDT order NOT alphabetical. | pe-imphash-signature-engineering §Pipeline |
| **imphash (extended)** | 128-bit MD5 hash-pivot key over normalized IDT entries; Mandiant 2014 specification. Lowercase DLL names; function names case-preserved. | pe-imphash-signature-engineering §Specification |
| **API hashing** | Attacker counter-technique #1 against imphash — replace static imports with runtime hash-resolution of function names; empties the IDT, defeats imphash matching. | pe-imphash-signature-engineering §Counter-techniques |
| **dynamic LoadLibrary** | Attacker counter-technique #2 against imphash — replace static imports with `LoadLibraryA` + `GetProcAddress` only; reduces IDT to two entries, defeats family clustering. | pe-imphash-signature-engineering §Counter-techniques |

## Cybersecurity — Wireshark promotion (raw→wiki, 2026-08-17)

Added 2026-08-17 from the Wireshark reference-PDF + teaching-note ingest that promotes the long-flagged `wireshark` raw stub (per rtfm-clark-downer §Out-of-scope #4) to three pages. Owners: the tool → wireshark; the display-filter language → wireshark-display-filter-grammar; the capture-filter language → bpf-berkeley-packet-filter. The `all_eq` / `any_ne` multi-value operators are the load-bearing, easy-to-miss members of the display grammar; the capture-vs-display boundary is the single most error-prone distinction.

| Term | Expansion / meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Wireshark** | Network-protocol analyzer ("microscope for packets"); capture-then-dissect GUI + `tshark` CLI. 10-menu surface; two-stage filter funnel; Follow-Stream / Conversations / Export-Objects / IO-Graphs as the core investigation actions. | wireshark |
| **tshark** | Wireshark's terminal twin; same dissectors + same display-filter grammar. `-Y` = display filter, `-f` = capture (BPF) filter. | wireshark §tshark |
| **Display filter** | Wireshark's post-capture, reversible query grammar over dissector field names (`ip.addr`, `tcp.flags.syn`); the *look-at* half of the funnel. Distinct language from BPF. | wireshark-display-filter-grammar |
| **all_eq (`===`) / any_ne (`!==`)** | Multi-value display-filter operators: `===` = *all* occurrences of a repeated field equal; `!==` = *all* differ. The reason `ip.addr != x` fails to exclude a host (plain `!=` is *any*-occurrence). | wireshark-display-filter-grammar §Comparison |
| **BPF (Berkeley Packet Filter)** | The *capture-filter* grammar — kernel/libpcap-level, applied before packets hit disk, **irreversible**. Primitives = qualifier (type/direction/protocol) + id (`host`/`net`/`port`). Byte-offset form `tcp[13] & 2`. The *keep* half of the funnel. | bpf-berkeley-packet-filter |
| **Capture filter** | The BPF filter box specifically (Wireshark start screen / Capture Options); registered as a pointer to distinguish it from the display filter. Drops are permanent. | bpf-berkeley-packet-filter |
| **tcpdump / WinDump** | The canonical headless BPF-capture tools (*nix / Windows port); capture on the target host to `.pcap`, read in Wireshark. WinDump = tcpdump-for-Windows. | bpf-berkeley-packet-filter §Tools |
| **Dump-tool family** | The four saved-capture sources on the reference sketch: Wireshark native (`.pcapng`/`.pcap`) · tcpdump · WinDump · Hex Dump. | wireshark §capture pipeline |
| **Follow TCP Stream** | Analyze → Follow — reassembles a bidirectional session into one transcript; where cleartext credentials fall out of an unencrypted `POST`. | wireshark §GUI actions |

---

## Proof theory layer (Mancosu-Galvan-Zach ingest, 2026-05-27)

Added 2026-05-27 from the [Mancosu, Galvan, Zach (2021) *Introduction to Proof Theory*](./proof-theory-mancosu-galvan-zach.md) ingest. These terms govern the metalogic tier of [logic-atomic-design](./logic-atomic-design.md) — the layer above Copi's symbolic logic. The new pages sit at level 8, domain 10 (logic), rooms 40-51.

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Hilbert's Program** | David Hilbert's 1920s foundational program — prove the consistency of infinitary classical mathematics by finitary metamathematics. Three-level distinction (ordinary math · proper formalized math · metamath). Strict form shut by [Gödel 1931](./godels-incompleteness.md) second incompleteness; partly rescued by [Gentzen 1936](./gentzens-proof-theory.md) via [ε₀-induction](./epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction.md). | [hilberts-program](./hilberts-program.md) |
| **Gentzen's Proof Theory** | Gerhard Gentzen's 1933-1938 work — [Gödel-Gentzen translation](./godel-gentzen-translation.md) (1933) · [natural deduction](./natural-deduction.md) NJ/NK (1935a) · [sequent calculus](./sequent-calculus.md) LJ/LK (1935b) · [cut-elimination Hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) (1935c) · [ε₀-consistency proof for PA](./consistency-of-peano-arithmetic.md) (1936/1938). Mnemonic **TNSCO**. | [gentzens-proof-theory](./gentzens-proof-theory.md) |
| **Natural Deduction (NM/NJ/NK)** | Gentzen's 1935 proof system in which each connective has an intro and elim rule pair; proofs are trees of formulas; some assumptions are discharged. NM = minimal, NJ = intuitionistic, NK = classical. Proofs match the way mathematicians actually argue ("assume A, derive B, conclude A→B"). | [natural-deduction](./natural-deduction.md) |
| **Sequent Calculus (LJ/LK)** | Gentzen's 1935 alternative proof system where the basic object is a sequent Γ ⊢ Δ (antecedent ⊢ succedent). Each connective has L and R rules; structural rules manage formula lists; cut rule packages lemma-use. LK = classical (Δ arbitrary), LJ = intuitionistic (\|Δ\| ≤ 1). | [sequent-calculus](./sequent-calculus.md) |
| **Normalization Theorem** | Prawitz 1965 (NJ) / Andou 1995 (NK): every natural-deduction proof can be effectively transformed into a *normal* proof — one with no detours (intro followed immediately by elim on the same formula). The ND analog of [Gentzen's Hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md). | [normalization-theorem](./normalization-theorem.md) |
| **Cut-Elimination Theorem / Hauptsatz** | Gentzen 1935: every LK / LJ proof can be effectively transformed into a cut-free proof. The structural payoff: [sub-formula-property](./sub-formula-property.md), decidability of propositional logic, consistency of pure first-order logic, [Herbrand's theorem](./godel-gentzen-translation.md). Mancosu et al. use *mix* (generalized cut) in the proof. | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) |
| **Sub-formula Property** | In a normal NJ proof of A from Γ, every formula appearing is a sub-formula of A or of some γ ∈ Γ. The dual: cut-free LK proofs have every formula a sub-formula of the end-sequent. The formal-logic embodiment of [TLP show-vs-say](./show-vs-say.md) and the [NEDF](./nedf-overview.md) Distinguisher discipline. | [sub-formula-property](./sub-formula-property.md) |
| **Detour (NJ/NK)** | An introduction rule for connective ⋆ immediately followed by the elimination rule for ⋆ applied to the introduced formula as major premise. The introduced formula was wasted. Normalization eliminates all detours. Corresponds to *cut* in the sequent calculus and to *β-redex* in the simply-typed λ-calculus (Curry-Howard). | [normalization-theorem](./normalization-theorem.md) |
| **Cut (LK/LJ)** | The sequent-calculus rule from Γ ⊢ Δ, C and C, Π ⊢ Λ inferring Γ, Π ⊢ Δ, Λ — formula C disappears in conclusion. Formalizes lemma-use. The only rule that breaks the sub-formula property. Eliminable by Gentzen's Hauptsatz. | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) |
| **Mix (proof-theory)** | Generalized cut handling all occurrences of the cut-formula at once. Mancosu et al. use mix instead of cut in the Hauptsatz proof to avoid case-explosion. Translation mix↔cut is straightforward. | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) §Why mix? |
| **Cut-rank** | Length of the longest cut-segment ending at a given cut inference. With *cut-degree* (number of logical symbols in cut-formula), forms the lexicographic complexity measure that the cut-elimination algorithm strictly decreases. | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) §Proof sketch |
| **Cut-degree** | Number of logical symbols in the cut-formula. Together with cut-rank, the well-founded measure for Hauptsatz termination. | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) §Proof sketch |
| **Detour conversion** | One of two conversion types in normalization. Transforms a sub-proof ending in a detour (intro then elim) into a shorter sub-proof using only the intro's premises. Strictly decreases cut-degree. | [normalization-theorem](./normalization-theorem.md) |
| **Permutation conversion** | The second conversion type in normalization. Pushes an elim rule past a preceding ∨E or ∃E case-analysis. Shortens cut-segments. Strictly decreases cut-rank. | [normalization-theorem](./normalization-theorem.md) |
| **Gödel-Gentzen translation** | Syntactic translation (·)' from classical to intuitionistic logic: p' = ¬¬p for atomic p; commutes with ∧/→/¬/∀; rewrites ∨ as ¬(¬·∧¬·) and ∃ as ¬∀¬. Theorem: PA ⊢ φ iff HA ⊢ φ'. Consequence: Con(HA) ⇒ Con(PA). | [godel-gentzen-translation](./godel-gentzen-translation.md) |
| **ε₀** | Least ordinal α with ω^α = α; limit of ω, ω^ω, ω^{ω^ω}, … The proof-theoretic ordinal of [PA](./hilberts-program.md). PA proves induction up to every β < ε₀ but cannot prove ε₀ is well-ordered. | [epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction](./epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction.md) |
| **Ordinal notation** | Finite syntactic object representing an ordinal < ε₀ in Cantor normal form (ω^β₁·n₁ + … + ω^βₖ·nₖ with βᵢ also ordinal notations). Primitive-recursive; well-foundedness provable combinatorially (with [Gödel's caveat](./godels-incompleteness.md) that PA can't prove its own ordinal's well-foundedness). | [epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction](./epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction.md) |
| **Proof-theoretic ordinal** | The least ordinal α such that a theory T cannot prove transfinite induction along α. For PA: ε₀. For predicative ACA: Γ₀ (Feferman-Schütte). For impredicative Π¹₁-CA₀: Bachmann-Howard ordinal. The schedule classifies arithmetic theories by induction-strength. | [epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction](./epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction.md) §The schedule |
| **Goodstein sequence** | A naturally-defined sequence on ℕ that always terminates at 0 (Goodstein 1944) — provable by ε₀-induction but **not provable in PA** (Kirby-Paris 1982). First natural arithmetic statement unprovable in PA. Prototype for "drills that look hopeless but terminate by reasons outside the substrate." | [epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction](./epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction.md) §Goodstein |
| **Cantor normal form** | Unique representation of ordinals < ε₀ as ω^β₁·n₁ + ω^β₂·n₂ + … + ω^βₖ·nₖ where β₁ > β₂ > … > βₖ ≥ 0 are themselves ordinals < ε₀ and n₁, …, nₖ are positive integers. The base case β = 0 makes the recursion terminate. | [epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction](./epsilon-zero-and-ordinal-induction.md) §Ordinal notations |
| **Consistency of PA (Gentzen 1936/1938)** | The proof: assign each PA-proof an ordinal notation < ε₀; define a reduction procedure that strictly decreases this ordinal; well-foundedness of ε₀ forces termination; terminal proof has only atomic cuts and no induction, by inspection cannot derive ⊥. Goes beyond [strict-finitary](./hilberts-program.md) but stays constructive. | [consistency-of-peano-arithmetic](./consistency-of-peano-arithmetic.md) |
| **Reductive vs general proof theory** | Prawitz 1971 distinction. *Reductive* proof theory: use proofs to reduce infinitary epistemology to finitary (Hilbert's goal). *General* proof theory: study proofs as mathematical objects without epistemic constraints (Gentzen's broader legacy). The wiki's encoder paradigm is general-proof-theory in spirit. | [gentzens-proof-theory](./gentzens-proof-theory.md) |
| **Proof-theoretic semantics** | The Dummett-Prawitz program: the meaning of a logical constant is determined by its [rules of use](./natural-deduction.md) (specifically its intro rule); the elim rule is justified by **harmony** with the intro rule. Anti-truth-conditional. Natively intuitionistic. Wiki ancestor of [NEDF](./nedf-overview.md) · [GRACE](./grace-overview.md) · [METER](./meter-overview.md). | [proof-theoretic-semantics](./proof-theoretic-semantics.md) |
| **Harmony (Dummett-Prawitz)** | The condition that the elim rule for ⋆ extracts no more than the intro rule deposits. Formally: detours can be reduced. Wiki ancestor of the *minimum-sufficient-information* discipline (GRACE's Read sets up exactly what Alternatives need). | [proof-theoretic-semantics](./proof-theoretic-semantics.md) §Harmony |
| **Tonk objection (Prior 1960)** | Hypothetical connective with ∨I-style intro and ∧E-style elim; combining the two collapses logic to triviality. Lesson: rules of use alone don't constitute meaning if harmony fails. Wiki application: protocols must be falsifiable; the wiki's "no parallel definitions, no overclaim" lint rule is the analog. | [proof-theoretic-semantics](./proof-theoretic-semantics.md) §Tonk |
| **Mid-sequent theorem** | For a cut-free LK proof of ⊢ ∃y₁…∃yₙ A: there is a *mid-sequent* with empty antecedent where everything above is quantifier-free and everything below is just ∃R + structural. Corollary: Herbrand's theorem (finitely many witness-tuples). | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) §Mid-sequent |
| **Herbrand's theorem** | Corollary of mid-sequent: if LK proves ⊢ ∃y₁…∃yₙ A(y₁,…,yₙ) with A quantifier-free, then there exist finitely many witness-tuples t̄₁, …, t̄ₖ (terms from the proof) such that ⊢ A(t̄₁), …, A(t̄ₖ) is provable propositionally. **Constructive witness extraction**. | [cut-elimination-hauptsatz](./cut-elimination-hauptsatz.md) §Herbrand |
| **Existence property (LJ)** | Stronger Herbrand for LJ: if LJ ⊢ ⊢ ∃x A(x) with A quantifier-free, there exists a *single* term t with LJ ⊢ ⊢ A(t). The intuitionistic constructive-witness property. | [sub-formula-property](./sub-formula-property.md) §Existence |
| **Disjunction property (LJ)** | If LJ ⊢ ⊢ A ∨ B, then LJ ⊢ ⊢ A or LJ ⊢ ⊢ B. The intuitionistic *winning-disjunct* property. Fails in LK. | [sub-formula-property](./sub-formula-property.md) §Existence |
| **Discharge (ND)** | The natural-deduction mechanism by which an assumption A introduced in a sub-proof can be *closed out* at an →I, ∨E, or ∃E step — so subsequent inferences depend on the new conditional/cased/witnessed conclusion but no longer on A. Formal-logic ancestor of the production-reception grammar pattern. | [natural-deduction](./natural-deduction.md) §Discharge |
| **NM / NJ / NK** | Gentzen's three natural-deduction systems for predicate logic. NM = minimal (no special rule for ⊥); NJ = intuitionistic (NM + ⊥-elim); NK = classical (NJ + classical absurdity rule). Subscript 0 (NM₀, NJ₀, NK₀) for the propositional fragments. | [natural-deduction](./natural-deduction.md) |
| **M₀ / J₀ / K₀ / M₁ / J₁ / K₁** | Mancosu et al.'s axiomatic-calculus counterparts to NM/NJ/NK. M = minimal, J = intuitionistic, K = classical; subscript 0 = propositional, subscript 1 = predicate. M₀ + PL11 (ex-falso) = J₀; J₀ + PL12 (DNE) = K₀. | [proof-theory-mancosu-galvan-zach](./proof-theory-mancosu-galvan-zach.md) §Ch 2 |
| **Conservativity** | A theory T' is conservative over T if every T-sentence provable in T' is already provable in T. The Gödel-Gentzen translation makes PA conservative over HA *for the translated formulas*. The wiki's lint discipline implements conservativity at the page level — new ingests must not change prior claims at the atomic-fact layer. | [godel-gentzen-translation](./godel-gentzen-translation.md) §Conservativity |
| **Suitable cut** | A cut that blocks reduction unless eliminated at the current step. Concept used in Mancosu et al. Ch 7-9 to make the reduction procedure deterministic. Strictly-decreasing-suitable-cuts is the engine of the consistency proof. | [consistency-of-peano-arithmetic](./consistency-of-peano-arithmetic.md) §Reduction-procedure |

## Cross-cutting cross-references

The proof-theory pages add new instance-rows to several existing wiki patterns:

- [composability-index](./composability-index.md) §confirmed unlocks gains 7 rows (sub-formula × Distinguisher × show-vs-say; cut-elimination × FRAME-FORGE × /lint; ε₀ × OK-Plateau × self-image; Gödel-Gentzen × ANT-countering; ND-discharge × I-utterance/SHIELD; Mancosu × Logic Atomic Design metalogic tier; intro/elim duality × proof-theoretic harmony × GRACE) + 2 candidate rows (mid-sequent × recognition-gym finitary bound; Gentzen reduction × wiki audit termination).
- [logic-atomic-design](./logic-atomic-design.md) gains a *metalogic tier* — proof-theoretic atoms (rule of inference, cut, sub-formula), molecules (intro/elim duality, harmony), organisms (normalization-as-pipeline, cut-elimination-as-pipeline), templates (ND tree, sequent tree), pages (Gentzen's Con(PA), the Gödel-Gentzen translation as a worked instance).
- [show-vs-say](./show-vs-say.md) gains *sub-formula property* as a precise formal-logic instance of TLP 4.121.
- [nedf-overview](./nedf-overview.md) gains its formal-logic home — the Distinguisher discipline is the encoder-layer instance of the sub-formula property.

## Graph theory layer (Axenovich ingest, 2026-05-27)

Added 2026-05-27 from Maria Axenovich's *Graph Theory* (KIT Lecture Notes 2020, 75 pp). These terms govern the formal graph-theory substrate now underlying [CAST](./cast-overview.md), DSA, CCNA networking, and the extremal / Ramsey / probabilistic mathematics layers. The hub page is graph-theory-overview; owner pages are the 11 pages under `wiki/graph-theory/`.

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **G = (V, E)** | A graph with vertex set V and edge set E ⊆ {uv : u,v ∈ V, u ≠ v}. Order = \|V\|; size = \|E\|. The foundation of every graph-theory result. | graph-theory-overview |
| **δ(G) / Δ(G)** | Minimum / maximum degree of G. The two fundamental degree parameters; appear in almost every structural theorem. | graph-theory-overview |
| **Handshake Lemma** | Σ deg(v) = 2\|E\| for any graph G. Immediate consequence: number of odd-degree vertices is always even. | graph-theory-overview |
| **κ(G) — vertex connectivity** | Minimum number of vertices whose removal disconnects G (or leaves one vertex). Measures how "robustly connected" G is. Inequality: κ(G) ≤ λ(G) ≤ δ(G). | graph-connectivity |
| **λ(G) — edge connectivity** | Minimum number of edges whose removal disconnects G. Satisfies κ(G) ≤ λ(G) ≤ δ(G) (Whitney 1932). | graph-connectivity |
| **Bridge / cut edge** | An edge whose removal increases the number of connected components. CAST vocabulary: the "bridge" CAST spoke about is formally a bridge in this sense. | graph-connectivity |
| **Cut vertex / articulation point** | A vertex whose removal increases the number of connected components. CAST vocabulary: "bottleneck node" = cut vertex. | graph-connectivity |
| **Block (graph)** | A maximal 2-connected subgraph (or a bridge, or an isolated vertex). CAST vocabulary: "cluster" = block. | graph-connectivity |
| **Menger's Theorem** | Local connectivity (max internally-disjoint paths from u to v) = global connectivity (min vertex/edge cut separating u from v). Dual form of max-flow min-cut. | graph-connectivity |
| **Matching / ν(G)** | A set of pairwise non-adjacent edges; ν(G) = maximum matching size. For bipartite graphs, ν(G) = τ(G) (König's Theorem). | matching-theory |
| **Hall's Marriage Theorem** | Bipartite G = (A ∪ B, E) has a matching saturating A ↔ for all S ⊆ A: \|N(S)\| ≥ \|S\|. The feasibility test for complete bipartite matching. | matching-theory |
| **König's Theorem** | In bipartite graphs: max matching size ν(G) = min vertex cover size τ(G). | matching-theory |
| **Planar graph** | A graph embeddable in the plane without edge crossings. Euler's formula: V − E + F = 2. Edge bound: E ≤ 3n − 6. No K₅ or K₃,₃ minor (Kuratowski). | planar-graphs |
| **χ(G) — chromatic number** | Minimum number of colors needed to color V(G) so adjacent vertices get distinct colors. Brooks: χ ≤ Δ unless G = Kₙ or odd cycle. | graph-colorings |
| **χ'(G) — chromatic index** | Minimum colors for edge-coloring (adjacent edges get distinct colors). Vizing: χ' ∈ {Δ, Δ+1}. | graph-colorings |
| **ω(G) / α(G)** | ω(G) = clique number (largest clique); α(G) = independence number (largest independent set). Perfect Graph Theorem: χ(G) = ω(G) for perfect graphs. | graph-colorings |
| **ex(n, H) — extremal number** | Maximum number of edges in an H-free graph on n vertices. Turán's theorem: ex(n, Kᵣ) = \|E(Tᵣ₋₁(n))\|. Erdős-Stone: asymptotic ex(n,H)/C(n,2) → (χ(H)−2)/(χ(H)−1). | extremal-graph-theory |
| **Turán graph Tᵣ(n)** | The complete balanced r-partite graph of order n — the densest Kᵣ₊₁-free graph. | extremal-graph-theory |
| **R(k) — Ramsey number** | Smallest n such that every 2-coloring of Kₙ has a monochromatic Kₖ. Bounds: 2^{k/2} ≤ R(k) ≤ 4^k. Known values: R(3)=6, R(4)=18. | ramsey-theory |
| **G(n,p) model** | Erdős-Rényi random graph — each of the C(n,2) possible edges included independently with probability p ∈ [0,1]. Threshold for connectivity: p = log n / n. Threshold for Hamiltonian cycle: p = (1+ε)log n / n. | random-graphs |
| **Probabilistic method** | To show that an object with some property exists, construct a random object and show Prob(property) > 0. Non-constructive; first used by Erdős to prove R(k) > 2^{k/2}. | random-graphs |
| **Max-flow min-cut (Ford-Fulkerson)** | max\|f\| = min c(S, S̄) for any network N = (G, s, t, c). Equivalent to Menger's theorem. Foundation of CCNA's "bottleneck = slowest link" intuition. | network-flows |
| **Flow number φ(G)** | Smallest k such that G has a nowhere-zero k-flow. Seymour 1981: every bridgeless graph has φ(G) ≤ 6. Tutte's conjecture: φ(G) ≤ 5. | network-flows |
| **Hamiltonian cycle** | A cycle visiting every vertex of G exactly once (spanning cycle). Dirac 1952: δ(G) ≥ n/2 ⟹ Hamiltonian. Fleischner 1974: G 2-connected ⟹ G² Hamiltonian. CAST palace walk = Hamiltonian path. | hamiltonian-cycles |
| **Szemerédi Regularity Lemma** | Every dense graph can be partitioned into a bounded number of ε-regular (quasi-random) pairs. Foundational in modern combinatorics and theoretical CS. | extremal-graph-theory |

---

## Second Language Acquisition (SLA) layer (Krashen + Lorayne + Wyner + Pinker ingest, 2026-05-29)

| Term | Short definition | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Coda loss (French)** | The historical deletion of the word-final syllable coda, and the positional rule it leaves behind: a consonant surfaces when it can occupy an **onset**, and is deleted in coda position. One event behind four systems — silent final letters, nasal vowels (a deleted nasal coda's residue on the preceding vowel), the mute agreement `-e`, and all linking. Registered 2026-08-28 from [PRISM](./prism-pattern-discovery.md) run #4 | [french-coda-loss](./french-coda-loss.md) |
| **Enchaînement** | An *already-pronounced* final consonant sliding onto the next word's vowel (`elle est` → /ɛ.lɛ/). Distinct from liaison, which resurrects a *silent* one; same audible result | [french-coda-loss](./french-coda-loss.md) |
| **h aspiré** | A silent `h` that nonetheless **blocks** elision and liaison (`le héros`, not *l'héros*) — as opposed to *h muet*, which allows both (`l'homme`). The case proving the rule operates on syllable **slots**, not on sounds | [french-coda-loss](./french-coda-loss.md) |
| **Acquisition** | Subconscious language development via comprehensible input; produces automatic fluent output. Distinct from Learning. | [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) |
| **Learning (SLA)** | Conscious knowledge about language (grammar rules, explicit study). Feeds the Monitor only; cannot become Acquisition. | [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) |
| **Acquisition-Learning Distinction** | Krashen Hypothesis 1: Acquisition and Learning are stored separately; only Acquisition drives real-time fluent production. | [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) |
| **Natural Order Hypothesis** | Krashen Hypothesis 2: grammatical morphemes are acquired in a fixed, predictable sequence regardless of instruction order. | [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) |
| **Monitor Hypothesis** | Krashen Hypothesis 3: consciously learned rules serve only as an editor (Monitor) on already-acquired utterances; they cannot generate. | [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) |
| **Comprehensible Input** | Language input the learner can understand (≥95% known words); the necessary condition for acquisition. | [comprehensible-input-protocol](./comprehensible-input-protocol.md) |
| **i+1** | Input level one step above current competence (i); the acquisition-productive zone. At i+2 (too far beyond), comprehension collapses and acquisition stops. | [comprehensible-input-protocol](./comprehensible-input-protocol.md) |
| **95% Rule** | Practical threshold: ~95% known words in a text = i+1 zone; below 90% = acquisition-nonproductive. | [comprehensible-input-protocol](./comprehensible-input-protocol.md) |
| **Affective Filter** | Krashen Hypothesis 5: psychological barrier (high anxiety, low motivation, low confidence) that blocks acquisition even when input is comprehensible. | [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) |
| **Language Acquisition Device (LAD)** | Chomsky/Pinker: hypothetical mental organ containing Universal Grammar; present in adults (less plastic than in children). | [language-instinct-pinker](./language-instinct-pinker.md) |
| **Universal Grammar (UG)** | Pinker/Chomsky: the species-wide deep grammatical structure common to all human languages; innate, not learned. | [language-instinct-pinker](./language-instinct-pinker.md) |
| **Critical Period** | Developmental window (roughly birth–puberty) of peak phonological plasticity; after puberty, accent acquisition requires more effort. Not a wall — a ramp. | [language-instinct-pinker](./language-instinct-pinker.md) |
| **Transfer (SLA)** | The facilitation of L2 acquisition by prior knowledge of a related language (positive transfer) or the interference of L1/prior-L2 patterns (negative transfer). | [language-family-clustering](./language-family-clustering.md) |
| **Language Family Clustering** | Strategic sequencing of L2 acquisition within related language families to maximize positive transfer and minimize per-language hours. | [language-family-clustering](./language-family-clustering.md) |
| **Substitute Word** | Lorayne's phonetic bridge technique: an English sound-alike phrase that serves as a mnemonic anchor for a foreign word's form and meaning. Owner page is [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md). | [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md) |
| **Phonetic Bridge** | Synonym for Substitute Word in the Neural OS vocabulary. The English sound-alike anchor that links a foreign word's pronunciation to its meaning. | [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md) |
| **SPM** | Sound → Phrase → Meaning: the three-step Substitute Word encoding protocol. The correct order is mandatory; reversal degrades encoding. | [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md) |
| **Onset Peg Alphabet** | A precompiled lookup table mapping each recurring word-onset (leading consonant cluster / frequent prefix) to one fixed, drilled-to-reflex concrete peg image — the Major System's architecture applied to syllable onsets, caching [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md)'s onset-anchoring so it is never re-derived. Candidate pending a falsifiable gate. | [onset-peg-alphabet](./onset-peg-alphabet.md) |
| **Sentence Mining** | Wyner Phase 3 technique: flagging unknown words encountered during native content reading; adding the whole sentence as a cloze Anki card rather than the isolated word. | [fluent-forever-wyner](./fluent-forever-wyner.md) |
| **PSSC** | Pronunciation-Spelling-Sound Correspondence — the targeted drill (minimal-pair audio + IPA + per-grapheme sound mapping) that builds phonological readiness *before* vocabulary work; Wyner's Days 1-7 bootstrap phase, not a synonym for the full 3-phase protocol arc (bootstrap → image-deck → sentence-mining). | [fluent-forever-wyner](./fluent-forever-wyner.md) |
| **No-translation rule** | Wyner: L2 vocabulary cards must use images (not L1 translations) as the meaning bridge; translation creates a two-link dependency that impedes direct L2 fluency. | [fluent-forever-wyner](./fluent-forever-wyner.md) |
| **Callan Method** | Forced-production speaking protocol (Robin Callan, 1960): teacher-speed questions (~240 wpm) asked twice, immediate full-sentence answer in a window too short for L1 translation — the gap admits only reflex production. Enters the wiki as a **donor of mechanics** (fixed-gap audio drill unit, timed say-cards, above-comfort input speed), not a new framework; revision and controlled vocabulary stay owned by [spaced-repetition](./spaced-repetition.md) and the french-1000 tier ladder. | [callan-method](./callan-method.md) |

## Marriage canon — covenant + empirical + sanctification layer (2026-05-30 mega-ingest)

Added 2026-05-30 from the four-book marriage-canon mega-ingest (Keller + Thomas + Gottman & Silver + Siegel & Bryson). Two doctrinal/devotional Protestant witnesses (Keller + Thomas) converge with a 16-year empirical longitudinal corpus (Gottman Love Lab, 91% divorce prediction). The Siegel-Bryson developmental-neuroscience source registers at the parenting substrate. Together: covenant + measurement + sanctification + developmental stack.

### Doctrinal layer (Keller + Thomas)

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Marriage covenant theology** | The 5-clause definition: marriage is (1) covenantal (2) one-flesh union (3) between man and woman (4) for sanctification through sacrificial self-gift (5) imaging Christ and the church. Replaces the previous 6-verse citation list at RAP §Theological. Mnemonic COSSI. | marriage-covenant-theology |
| **Marriage-as-sanctification** | Thomas's central thesis: marriage's primary purpose is character formation (holiness), not happiness; friction-with-spouse is the instrument. Structurally sister of [ericsson-peak](./ericsson-peak.md) deliberate-practice at the character substrate (5th-substrate instance). 3 mechanisms: Cleansing (spouse sees un-edited self) · Struggle (difficulty is the agent) · Forgiveness (recurring practice not single event). | marriage-as-sanctification |
| **Covenant > feeling** | Keller's central polemic against romantic-feeling paradigm: love measured not by how much you want to receive but by how much you give. The marriage vow is the test that love has reached marriage level. | marriage-covenant-vs-feeling |
| **Spirit-filled submission** | Eph 5:21 is the introductory clause to Paul's entire marriage paragraph (the last clause of the *be filled with the Spirit* sentence beginning at v.18). Mutual Spirit-empowered submission is the power source for the asymmetric calls in 5:22-33. Without 5:21, the rest is moralism without fuel. Theological owner of the empirical accept-influence (Gottman Principle 4). | spirit-filled-submission |
| **Loving the stranger** | Keller Ch 5 + Hauerwas: "we never know whom we marry; we just think we do." Central practical task of marriage is to love the stranger your spouse becomes and is revealed to be. Theological owner of the empirical love-maps (Gottman Principle 1). | loving-the-stranger |
| **Marriage as spiritual friendship** | Keller Ch 4: the 'ezer of Gen 2:18 is friend before role; marriage's mission (Eph 5:26-27 *to present her holy*) is mutual help becoming who-God-created-each-spouse-to-be. | marriage-as-spiritual-friendship |
| **Sex in covenant** | Keller Ch 8: sex is whole-life self-giving; the biblical sex ethic confines genital intimacy to covenant. Christianity is "the most body-positive religion." Rejects sex-as-appetite, sex-as-dirty, sex-as-self-expression. | sex-in-covenant |
| **Crucible (Thomas)** | Thomas's central metaphor for marriage as smelting vessel: Cleansing · Reconciliation-sign · Unedited witness · Call to holiness · Intimacy school · Burden-bearing · Lifelong perseverance · Exposure that forgives. Mnemonic CRUCIBLE. | thomas-sacred-marriage |
| **Covenant-floor alignment block** *(extended)* | Mandatory header block on every wiki page citing relational threat substrate. **2026-05-30 update**: now points to marriage-covenant-theology (Keller + Thomas as 2 named doctrinal owners) rather than recapitulating the 6-verse list. | marriage-covenant-theology §Mandatory alignment block |

### Empirical layer (Gottman 7 Principles + Love Lab)

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Sound Relationship House** | Gottman's integrating diagram for the 7 Principles: 7 floors + 2 weight-bearing walls (Trust + Commitment). Walls = covenant substrate; floors = day-to-day algorithms operating on the substrate. **6th sister of the atomic-design lens family** at the relational substrate. | sound-relationship-house |
| **Love Maps** | Gottman Principle 1 — continuously-updated mutual knowledge of the spouse as a person. Architecturally a **spouse-encoded NEDF deck** continuously updated, with 16-year empirical validation. 20 canonical slots; currency floor ≥ 15 of 20 weekly. Operationalises RAP §Layer 3 TRANSPARENCY. Theological sister: Keller "loving the stranger." | love-maps |
| **Fondness and admiration system** | Gottman Principle 2 — daily expression of valuing and respecting the spouse; **direct antidote to contempt**. Sustains the **5:1 positive-to-negative ratio** during conflict (Gottman's stability threshold; < 0.8:1 predicts divorce). | fondness-and-admiration-system |
| **Bids · Turning toward · Turn away · Turn against** | Gottman Principle 3 (Driver-Gottman 2004): every interaction contains a bid for attention/affection/humor/support; partner turns toward (deposit), away (withdrawal), or against (toxic withdrawal). Newlyweds at 86% turn-toward → still married after 6 years; 33% → divorced. Operationalises RAP §Layer 2 BUDGET as `bids_received / bids_acknowledged`. | turning-toward-bids |
| **Accept influence** | Gottman Principle 4. Empirical: men who refuse wife's influence → **81% marital meltdown** (Coan et al. 1997). Wives already accept influence at high rates; bottleneck is overwhelmingly male resistance. Convergent with Eph 5:21 mutual Spirit-empowered submission and Kathy Keller's Ch 6 servant-headship reading. | accept-influence |
| **Perpetual vs solvable conflict** | Gottman Ch 7 diagnostic. **69% of conflicts in happy marriages are perpetual** (fundamental personality/value differences that will not be solved); only 31% are solvable. Different conflict types require **opposite** protocols. Misdiagnosis is the central error: DRIFTING = misdiagnosed-solvable; CRISIS = misdiagnosed-gridlocked. Sister of Cynefin classify-then-route primitive. | perpetual-vs-solvable-conflict |
| **Solve solvable problems** | Gottman Principle 5 — the 5-step protocol applicable only to the 31% solvable conflicts: Soft startup · Repair attempts · Self-soothe · Compromise · Tolerate faults. Mnemonic SRSCT. | solve-solvable-problems |
| **Gridlock-and-dreams** | Gottman Principle 6. Gridlock is not failure-to-solve — it is **dreams underneath the surface conflict** that are identity-load-bearing. Each gridlock is a **dyadic Rung-2 paradigm conflict** (meadows-12-leverage-points sister). Protocol: detect dream → describe → dialogue (don't try to resolve) → make peace. | gridlock-and-dreams |
| **Shared meaning / marriage microculture** | Gottman Principle 7. Marital micro-culture built from 4 pillars: Rituals · Roles · Goals · Symbols (mnemonic RRGS). Resilience reservoir for hard years; softens conflict intensity; prevents gridlock. Sister of marriage-covenant-theology §Clause 5 iconic and meadows-12-leverage-points Rung 3 system-goals. | shared-meaning-marriage-culture |
| **Four Horsemen of the Marital Apocalypse (CCDS)** | Gottman's strongest divorce-prediction signature: **Criticism → Contempt → Defensiveness → Stonewalling** in escalating sequence. When all 4 present, 4-year divorce prediction > 90%. **Contempt is the single strongest predictor.** Owner of the post-formation degradation kill-chain at the covenant substrate — sister to Greene's pre-formation intrusion kill-chain. Together they form the **covenant-substrate kill-chain pair**. | four-horsemen-of-marital-apocalypse |
| **Harsh startup** | Gottman's opening-of-conflict signature: 96% of conversations beginning with criticism/contempt/sarcasm end badly (Carrère & Gottman 1999, 3-minute prediction). Antidote: **soft startup** (4-component: describe + feel + need + politely). Sister of i-utterance-protocol (production-grammar) and connect-and-redirect (child-substrate sister). | harsh-startup |
| **Repair attempt** | Any move (humor · take responsibility · soothe · sidetrack · reframe · touch) aimed at de-escalation during conflict. Distressed couples don't make fewer attempts — they **fail to catch them**. Acceptance-side asymmetry is the load-bearing variable. Theological sister: Thomas Ch 9 *Falling Forward* (recurring forgiveness practice). Mnemonic TRY-CATCH. | repair-attempts |
| **5:1 magic ratio** | Gottman's stability threshold: **5 positive interactions for every 1 negative during conflict** = stable; **< 0.8:1** = divorce track within 4 years. Umbrella vital sign for the relational substrate. | fondness-and-admiration-system |
| **DPA (Diffuse Physiological Arousal)** | Gottman's term for the stonewalling-flood state: heart rate > 100 bpm, cognitive faculties degrade, upstairs brain offline. **Same neurological mechanism as Siegel's flipped lid** at the adult substrate. Recovery: **20-minute break** for physiological return; breaks shorter don't recover, longer than 24h risks avoidance. | four-horsemen-of-marital-apocalypse §Stonewalling |

### Developmental layer (Siegel-Bryson Whole-Brain Child)

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Integration (whole-brain)** | Siegel-Bryson's central primitive: linking distinct brain regions to work as a coordinated whole rather than fighting each other. Instantiated across **5 axes**: Horizontal (L↔R) · Vertical (upstairs↔downstairs) · Memory (implicit↔explicit) · Self (parts of self) · Me-We (self↔other). | whole-brain-integration |
| **River of well-being** | Siegel's mental-health image: a peaceful river between two banks — **chaos** (out of control) and **rigidity** (over-controlled). Health = staying in the flow; pathology = pulled toward either bank. Closes the **seconds-to-minutes** layer in the wiki's temporal substrate stack (between [PULSE](./pulse-overview.md) seconds and [BDNF](./bdnf-and-neurogenesis.md) days). Sister of Meadows resilience corridor. | river-of-well-being |
| **Upstairs brain / Downstairs brain** | Siegel-Bryson's vertical-integration model. **Upstairs** = cerebral cortex (planning, empathy, morality) — structurally unfinished until **mid-twenties**. **Downstairs** = brain stem + limbic + amygdala (fight/flight, raw emotion) — built at birth. Provides the **missing developmental floor** for [skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md) L9 Coagulation. | upstairs-downstairs-brain |
| **Flipped lid / Amygdala hijack** | When emotional charge fires sufficiently, the **amygdala blocks the staircase to the upstairs brain** (Siegel's "baby gate latches"). Upstairs offline; only soothing and time restore access. **Same mechanism as Gottman's DPA stonewalling** at the adult relational substrate. | upstairs-downstairs-brain §Flipped lid |
| **Upstairs vs downstairs tantrum** | Siegel-Bryson's operationally-critical distinction. **Upstairs tantrum** = child strategically using emotion as leverage; can stop instantly when conditions change. Response: **firm boundary, named consequence.** **Downstairs tantrum** = child flooded, upstairs hijacked, no strategic capacity. Response: **nurture, soothe, connect first.** Applying wrong protocol is cruel or reinforcing. | upstairs-downstairs-brain §Tantrum classification |
| **Connect and redirect** | Siegel-Bryson Strategy #1 — **the highest-leverage parenting move.** Right-brain attunement (tone, touch, presence, "feel felt") **before** left-brain redirection. Order is non-negotiable. **5th confirmed instance** of the production-reception-grammar-pair pattern (crossing N=5 battle-tested threshold). Adult sister: soft startup. | connect-and-redirect |
| **Name it to tame it** | Siegel-Bryson Strategy #2. **Labeling an emotion in words literally down-regulates amygdala BOLD signal** (Lieberman 2007 fMRI). Parenting move: child retells the story; parent facilitates. Provides the **developmental-neuroscience mechanism floor** for the wiki's [ANT-counter](./ants-and-lies-of-learning.md) discipline. | name-it-to-tame-it |
| **Attunement / Feel felt** | Siegel-Bryson's term for the right-brain-to-right-brain connection that precedes effective parenting (and adult relating). Letting the other person know their interior is received. Mechanism: mirror-neuron resonance + autonomic co-regulation. | connect-and-redirect §Attunement |
| **Heart, not behavior** | Hubbard's discipline thesis (Prov 4:23 — biblical *heart* = inner disposition, **not** the [HEART](./heart-overview.md) encoder). Misbehavior is a symptom; the heart is the root. Correcting only visible behavior is the discipline analog of the [ok-plateau](./ok-plateau.md) — never compounds into character. Parent-side operational doctrine for the EGW character floor (Layer 0 of tracking-child-skills-development). | hubbard-dont-make-me-count-to-three |
| **Heart-probing questions** | Hubbard's 4-question discipline protocol: *what did you do · was it right or wrong · what should you have done instead · what will happen if you continue.* Guided self-reflection → self-conviction, not imposed shame. Structurally the left-brain **redirect content** for connect-and-redirect; sibling of name-it-to-tame-it on the conscience channel. | heart-probing-questions |
| **Mindsight** | Siegel's term for the capacity to perceive one's own mind and the mind of another (self-awareness + empathy). Biologically anchored in middle prefrontal cortex. Strategy #10. | mindsight-wheel-of-awareness |
| **Wheel of awareness** | Siegel's diagram for mindsight: **hub** of awareness with **spokes** of attention pointing to **rim** points (sensations · images · feelings · thoughts · memories · perceptions · desires). Pathology = stuck on the rim; practice = returning to the hub. Hub ≈ middle prefrontal cortex. | mindsight-wheel-of-awareness |
| **SIFT** | Siegel-Bryson Strategy #9 — 4-slot scan of internal experience: **S**ensations · **I**mages · **F**eelings · **T**houghts. The rim-scanner protocol for the wheel of awareness. First instance of [HEART](./heart-overview.md) applied **inward** (encoder grammar for self-perception). | sift-internal-attention |
| **Feel vs am** | Siegel-Bryson Strategy #8 discipline: *"I **feel** lonely right now"* (state, transient) vs *"I **am** lonely"* (trait, identity). Cognitive-developmental sister of [ANT-counter](./ants-and-lies-of-learning.md) *Labels* distortion. | mindsight-wheel-of-awareness §Feel vs am |
| **Mental staircase (vertical integration)** | Siegel-Bryson's image for the upstairs-downstairs connection. Integration = building and reinforcing the staircase through practiced experiences requiring upstairs use (decision-making, perspective-taking, impulse-delay). | upstairs-downstairs-brain |
| **River of well-being chaos bank / rigidity bank** | The two failure-modes Siegel names. **Chaos bank**: emotional flood, reactive, no order. **Rigidity bank**: emotion denied, controlled-stagnant, no adaptation. Visited daily — health is the *return-time*. | river-of-well-being |

|---|---|---|

Confirms trust-boundary-overflow thesis at a 4th layer. | [composability-index](./composability-index.md) §Confirmed |

Resolves long-standing wiki silence on pedagogical limits. | upstairs-downstairs-brain §Developmental floor |

## Money defensive-layer terms

Added 2026-06-16 — the four owner pages that fill the named-but-empty defensive/operational slots of personal-finance-six-anchors and the deferred macro layer of money-learning-architecture. These pages were authored, not source-ingested; jurisdiction-specific tax figures are flagged for verification on their owner page.

| Term | Definition | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Tax layer** | The `− Tax` term of `Profit = Revenue − Cost − Risk − Tax` treated as a cross-cutting *layer under the anchors* (firing at Door / Engine / Investment / throughput), not a 7th anchor. Grounded in Georgian regimes. | tax-layer-georgia |
| **Small Business Status (Georgia)** | Georgian Individual-Entrepreneur regime: 1% of turnover up to GEL 500k/year, 3% above (rs.ge, 2026-06-16). Prohibited list names **"consulting (including tax consultants)"** but **not IT/software/programming** — so *building* software qualifies for 1%, *advising* doesn't; the fork is definitional by GNC 006-2016 activity code / Decree N415. | tax-layer-georgia |
| **Micro Business (Georgia)** | 0% income tax for an individual under GEL 30k gross, no staff, not VAT-registered; same professional-services exclusion as Small Business; GEL 10 status fee. | tax-layer-georgia |
| **Virtual Zone Person (VZP)** | Georgian company (LLC) status for IT services delivered abroad; ~0% on exported-IT profit, tax at dividend distribution. Separate company track from the IE statuses. *Confirm current rules.* | tax-layer-georgia |
| **VAT threshold (Georgia)** | Mandatory VAT registration once taxable transactions exceed **GEL 100,000 over any continuous 12 months** (Tax Code, VAT chapter); 18% rate. A *second line* independent of the regime ceilings — it runs alongside the 1% Small-Business tax in the 100k–500k band; the 1% is **not** a VAT exemption. Fixed-tax-activity income is excluded from the count. | tax-layer-georgia |
| **Productive vs parasitic debt** | The asset/liability distinction applied to *borrowed* money: productive debt funds a cash-flowing asset (net positive after service); parasitic debt funds consumption (drains the Door). "Parasitic debt" is DeMarco's term. | debt-and-leverage |
| **Avalanche vs snowball** | The two canonical repayment orderings for multiple debts: avalanche = highest interest rate first (math-optimal); snowball = smallest balance first (momentum-optimal). Route by completion history. | debt-and-leverage |
| **The gambling test (debt)** | Diagnostic for whether a loan is a bet: *who rolls the die — me, or something I do not control?* Rate-exogenous slope (see idle-resource-is-adversarial) → bet; rate-endogenous → amplification. Carries the correction that debt is **worse** than a bet on two counts — the cost is certain where a stake's is not, and the loss is uncapped where a stake's is bounded. Registered 2026-07-29. | debt-and-leverage §Who rolls the die |
| **Expense return routing (Deduct · Recover · Convert · Accept)** | The four dispositions a *big* expense is routed to before booking, and the rule that only two of them yield income. **Deduct** = the tax shield (tax-layer-georgia); **Recover** = resale/points/exchange; **Convert** = produce a storable artifact that earns (idle-resource-is-adversarial §Spending a big expense into an artifact); **Accept** = its job is regeneration, so no return is possible. The disposition is chosen by the expense's **job**, not its size; forcing Convert onto a regeneration expense destroys its job. Named *Deduct*, not *Shield*, because Shield is the Risk anchor in personal-finance-six-anchors. Registered 2026-09-01. | personal-resource-inventory §The return decision |
| **Amortize for totals, never for extremes** | The rule governing when a lumpy expense may be smoothed into a monthly average: averaging is exact for any metric that is a *sum* (three-year net worth moved ≤1.2% across every phase) and wrong for any metric read off a *single worst period* (the cash floor moved 3.6×, peak monthly burn by 55%). The error lands entirely on min/max metrics and is approximately the cluster size, so a lumpy line earns its own cycle only when one cluster is a meaningful fraction of the floor. Corollary: a recurring outflow carries two levers — the **amount** (meadows-12-leverage-points rung 12) and the **phase** (rung 10) — and smoothing erases the second. Measured, not argued: `pages/expense-cycle-test.flow`, 8 passing `expect` lines. Registered 2026-09-01. | financial-prognosis-model §Amortizing a lumpy expense |
| **Four-verb risk taxonomy** | Every risk routes to exactly one of **Avoid · Reduce · Retain · Transfer**; the retain/transfer line is "can the Safe self-fund it?" | risk-and-insurance |
| **Shield-tracks-Engine calibration** | The Shield (risk-protection) is sized to Engine surface area; the right Shield is the smallest one covering every net-worth-halving event, not the maximum. | risk-and-insurance |
| **Fiat money** | Currency that is legal tender by decree, not commodity-backed; supply expandable at near-zero cost, which is why its *store-of-value* function decays. | monetary-system-what-is-money |
| **Cantillon effect** | Inflation as a *transfer*: whoever receives newly-created money first spends at old prices; late-receivers (wage-earners, cash-savers) find prices already risen. The mechanism that makes idle cash the most-taxed position. | monetary-system-what-is-money |
| **Inflation-as-implicit-tax** | Inflation reframed as a tax on holding the currency — levied with no vote and no line item — the implicit twin of the explicit rake in tax-layer-georgia. | monetary-system-what-is-money |

## Money simulation layer

Added 2026-06-19 — the personal-finance-six-anchors map expressed as a runnable stocks-and-flows model and exercised in FlowLoom. These terms are owned by the quantified-twin page; the anchor page stays the structural index.

| Term | Definition | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Financial Prognosis Model** | The `.flow` stocks-and-flows simulation of the user's six-anchor finances over a 3-year arc (3 stocks: Cash · Assets · LocalExpenses); run/swept/solved to rank levers. | financial-prognosis-model |
| **FlowLoom** | Text-first system-dynamics studio (`~/code/flowloom`) where the `.flow` text is canonical and the diagram/plots/R-B loop analysis are derived; CLI verbs `run · summary · sweep · sensitivity · solve · explain · check · compare`; since 2026-08-23 the language has `switch` (on/off policy params), `scenario` lines (named override sets in the text) and `previous`/`delay_fixed` pipeline delays for discrete monthly models, plus `sim method=map` (stock += change per step — a difference equation in its own vocabulary, no `× dt`). | financial-prognosis-model |
| **Freedom line** | The readout `freedom = passive − Expenses`; crosses zero when passive income alone covers the cost of living. Governed by `creepCap`/`yield`, not income. | financial-prognosis-model |
| **Lifestyle-creep ceiling (creepCap)** | The model knob for the share of income the local lifestyle drifts toward; the single most controllable lever over the freedom line. The dynamic form of the lifestyle-creep trap. | financial-prognosis-model |
| **USD-expense split** | Two-currency expense structure: a local GEL bucket that creeps with income plus `usdExpense = usdSpend * fx` (imports priced in USD); makes currency risk leak past income into the freedom line. | financial-prognosis-model |

## Jurisdiction of Correct (epistemology, 2026-07-04)

Added 2026-07-04 — authored from a conversation on *why know the truth if it isn't effective?*. One framework term; the full definition lives on the owner page.

| Term | Definition | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Jurisdiction of Correct** | Epistemic framework: the word *correct* names three different things, and truth's authority is domain-specific — absolute over **means**, none over **ends**. Three poles: **false convention** (norm mistaken for truth → drop), **true law with a delayed bill** (real causal law whose cost arrives late → keep), **terminal want** (efficiency verdict aimed at an end → no jurisdiction). Two diagnostics — the **mechanism-cost test** (break it: ring vs. thud) separates law from convention; the **reflective-endorsement test** (pay the bill in full, still endorse?) separates a terminal want from **akrasia**. | [truth-vs-effectiveness](./truth-vs-effectiveness.md) |

## Giordano/Kozarenko Mnemotechnics + Konnikova layer (GMS ingest, 2026-07-10)

Added 2026-07-10 from a four-source ingest: Kozarenko's GMS instructor syllabus (`GMS_V.Kozarenko.pdf`) + the graded distance-course (`Мнемотехника шаг за шагом.pdf`) — both the same Giordano school as the 2026-06-28 [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) original ingest — plus Maria Konnikova's *Mastermind: How to Think Like Sherlock Holmes* (2013) and *The Mnemonics Mind And Memory Improvement For Adults* (2020). The Kozarenko/Giordano terms are a **source layer feeding existing owners**, not a rival framework — no new encoder acronym is minted here (`/validate-idea` verdict, 2026-06-28, reconfirmed 2026-07-10).

**Tier-1 gap-fill addition (same day, glossary-warden pass):** three new pages — [spatial-coding](./spatial-coding.md), [four-level-blocks](./four-level-blocks.md), [prose-memorization](./prose-memorization.md) — added 8 more terms to this layer (Spatial Coding, Компас, Треугольная ассоциация, Стикер, Four-Level Block, Тематический стикер, Образный конспект, Метод сжатия). **Компас** is a Cyrillic-keyed disambiguation against the unrelated **Genius Compass** instrument (genius-compass) and the trig quadrant mnemonic ([eye-movement-and-compass-mnemonics](./eye-movement-and-compass-mnemonics.md)) — no collision, no rename needed. **Тематический стикер** is used on both [four-level-blocks](./four-level-blocks.md) (owner) and [prose-memorization](./prose-memorization.md) (consumer, links only).

**Phrase-layer gap-fill addition (same day, continuing glossary-warden pass):** the new page [phrase-based-acquisition](./phrase-based-acquisition.md) (GMS phrase-level language-acquisition layer) and a new §Phonetic image-codes section on [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) add 4 more terms: Речевой штамп, Фразовый эталон, Перевод по аналогии, Фонетические образные коды (Phonetic image-codes). **Составной ярлык** (compound label for grammar paradigms) is deliberately **not** registered: [phrase-based-acquisition](./phrase-based-acquisition.md) itself frames it as an application of the container-with-parts primitive it attributes to [spatial-coding](./spatial-coding.md) / [four-level-blocks](./four-level-blocks.md), not a new owned construct ("an application, not a new primitive" — its own section heading). Flag for a future pass: those two owner pages do not yet document the specific syllable-segmentation mechanism the составной ярлык paragraph describes (a documentation-completeness gap, not a naming collision — no rename needed).

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **БЦК** (Буквенно-Цифровой Код) | Kozarenko's Cyrillic phonetic number-alphabet — two consonants per digit (Löser-derived); the Cyrillic *instance* of the [Major System](./mnemonic-methods-master.md), not a rival to it. In prose since the 2026-06-28 ingest but registered here for the first time. | [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) §БЦК |
| **ТОО** (Таблица опорных образов) | Table of Support-Images — Kozarenko's self-generating synthetic-loci system: ~900 numerically-addressed cells grown from the 100 БЦК number-image codes via free-association + part-extraction; no photography or walked route required. | [table-of-support-images](./table-of-support-images.md) |
| **МНА** (Метод Наводящих Ассоциаций) | Method of Guiding Associations — Kozarenko's named seven-technique bundle for converting one abstract/unpicturable word into one concrete picturable image. A named *instance* of the abstract→concrete move owned by [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md), not a redefinition of it. | [method-of-guiding-associations](./method-of-guiding-associations.md) |
| **GMS** (Giordano Memorization System) | Source-name / school reference for Kozarenko's instructor-syllabus course (mnemonikon.ru, 1990–2020). Registered as a source-name, not a framework acronym — it enters the wiki feeding existing owners. | [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) |
| **Затирание** ("erasure") | Named failure mode: a reused image-code in the linking/background role of a chain silently overwrites the prior occurrence's forward link at encode time — undetectable until recall. The sequence-encoding twin of CAST's animal-collision rule ([nodes-and-edges](./nodes-and-edges.md) §External validation). | [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) §The "never chain image-codes" failure mode |
| **Приём возврата** (return technique) | The structural fix for **Затирание**: attach a repeating code as an isolated element on a branch of the *previous* image, never chain two repeat-codes directly. | [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) §The fix |
| **Motivation to Remember (MTR)** | Konnikova's mechanism: motivation matters at the point of **encoding**, not retrieval — no amount of retrieval-side motivation compensates for unmotivated intake. | [memory-is-residue-of-thought](./memory-is-residue-of-thought.md) |
| **Brain Attic** | Konnikova's governing metaphor for memory: a curated (not passive) store where the hippocampus is the entry point and only actively-considered material consolidates to the cortex. Names the **junk attic** failure mode (vivid imagery bolted onto unattended material). | [memory-is-residue-of-thought](./memory-is-residue-of-thought.md) |
| **S-O-I-E** | Konnikova's four-element observation protocol: Selective · Objective · Inclusive (+absence) · Engaged. The "deduction" built on top of it is Konnikova's colloquial/abductive usage, disambiguated from the formal system owned by [methods-of-deduction](./methods-of-deduction.md). | [observation-before-deduction](./observation-before-deduction.md) |
| **Name-Face Fast-Encode** | Tier-2 sub-5-second primitive: fuse a stranger's name + one prominent feature into a single Distinguisher-bearing hybrid word at first meeting; feeds [HEART](./heart-overview.md)'s Recognition doorway on recurrence. | [name-face-fast-encode](./name-face-fast-encode.md) |
| **Spatial Coding** (Пространственное кодирование) | Encoder primitive, not a storage method: uses the *position* of an image inside a locus or association (quadrant, reading direction, triangle geometry) as an extra information channel riding on top of images already placed — carries order, a datum, or an arithmetic operation, at no extra encoding cost. | [spatial-coding](./spatial-coding.md) |
| **Компас** (Kozarenko card-quadrant technique) | Splits each locus into 4 quadrants (top/bottom/left/right), one suit per quadrant, with rank read off the [Major System](./mnemonic-methods-master.md) number-image code — a position-based alternative to [PAO](./person-action-object-system.md) for card memorization. Distinct from the unrelated **Genius Compass** instrument (genius-compass, MURI/MURA/MUDA) and the trig quadrant mnemonic in [eye-movement-and-compass-mnemonics](./eye-movement-and-compass-mnemonics.md) — do not conflate; the Cyrillic key disambiguates. | [spatial-coding](./spatial-coding.md) §Channel 2 |
| **Треугольная ассоциация** (triangular association) | Spatial-arithmetic sub-primitive of Spatial Coding: images hung beneath a central image in a triangle encode a formula — the two bottom images multiply, the top image is divided by the bottom; vertical placement = addition, horizontal = subtraction. | [spatial-coding](./spatial-coding.md) §Channel 3 |
| **Стикер** (empty support-image) | An auxiliary image pressed into service purely as a container/locus for later chaining — carries no information of its own, distinct from a loaded association-base. The building block of the four-level block. | [four-level-blocks](./four-level-blocks.md) |
| **Four-Level Block** (Блок стикеров) | Kozarenko's second self-generating addressable-loci store: 125 three-digit-addressed loci (5 branches × 5 stickers × 5 parts) nested by Matryoshka onto one thematic collecting image; spun up per-topic, sibling of [ТОО](./table-of-support-images.md)'s ~900-cell number-indexed table. Counts and the automaticity gate per [skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md). | [four-level-blocks](./four-level-blocks.md) |
| **Тематический стикер** (thematic sticker) | The 26th collecting image that heads a four-level block and symbolizes a textbook section's topic; its parts hold the section's "dry residue" outline. Shared vocabulary with [prose-memorization](./prose-memorization.md), which uses the same collecting image to head a chapter — owned here, not redefined there. | [four-level-blocks](./four-level-blocks.md) |
| **Образный конспект** (image-outline) | Prose-memorization's default target for factual text: a two-axis image-outline where the vertical (Matryoshka) axis holds paragraph meaning-sequence and the horizontal (return-technique) axis holds exact embedded facts. | [prose-memorization](./prose-memorization.md) |
| **Метод сжатия** (compression method) | The base move under every prose case: a text fragment compresses to a short verbal title (словесная опора), which compresses again to one visual image (смысловая опора) via [method-of-guiding-associations](./method-of-guiding-associations.md). | [prose-memorization](./prose-memorization.md) |
| **Речевой штамп** (speech-stamp) | The automatized, reflexive form of a memorized phrase — fires without deliberation once **anticipation** and **reminiscence** both appear; the crossing-point onto the automaticity ladder ([skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md)). Distinct from **Фразовый эталон**, its pre-automatization state. | [phrase-based-acquisition](./phrase-based-acquisition.md) §The речевой штамп protocol |
| **Фразовый эталон** (phrase-standard) | A phrase memorized verbatim as a single unit but not yet automatized — effortful verbatim recall still counts as this, not yet a **Речевой штамп**. | [phrase-based-acquisition](./phrase-based-acquisition.md) §The речевой штамп protocol |
| **Перевод по аналогии** (translate-by-analogy) | Retrieval-side technique: produce an unmet sentence by adapting the nearest memorized reference phrase ("how a native speaker says it in this situation") rather than composing from grammar rules. | [phrase-based-acquisition](./phrase-based-acquisition.md) |
| **Фонетические образные коды** (Phonetic image-codes) | A БЦК-sibling fixed-image-code alphabet for foreign-language pronunciation/spelling — one image per IPA transcription sign or hiragana, written directly onto the meaning-image (*образ-значение*); a temporary scaffold dropped once the direct образ→произношение link forms. One more *instance* of the [Major System](./mnemonic-methods-master.md) pattern (sibling to БЦК), not a redefinition of it. | [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) §Phonetic image-codes |

**Not registered (human decision, 2026-07-10).** **System Holmes / System Watson** — Konnikova's illustrative nicknames for the slow/fast dual-process distinction. Kept as attributed narrative devices only; deliberately *not* minted as wiki terms or as a parallel name for Kahneman's System 1 / System 2 (would collide with the existing dual-process vocabulary). See [observation-before-deduction](./observation-before-deduction.md).
