## Yagodkin/Advance Mnemonics layer (Учись учиться ingest, 2026-07-16)

Added 2026-07-16 from Ягодкин Н.А. & Згода А.Н., *Учись учиться* (2023) — the Russian **Advance** training centre (Николай Ягодкин / Александр Згода), a **sibling and rival** to the Kozarenko/Giordano school above, not a descendant of it. Like Kozarenko, Advance enters the wiki as a **source feeding existing owners**, not a new framework or encoder — no new acronym at framework level (verdict recorded on [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md)). Four new owner pages: [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) (lineage Facade + doctrine map), [cognitive-house-model](./cognitive-house-model.md) (Advance's mind-architecture metaphor), [storm-and-siege-protocol](./storm-and-siege-protocol.md) (Advance's Штурм/Осада scheduling layer), [vocabulary-word-type-routing](./vocabulary-word-type-routing.md) (Advance's per-word-type routing table, including МегаЛоция). Advance's language-acquisition theory is recorded as a **rival account to Krashen** on [krashen-sla-hypotheses](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md) §Rival account — noted, not resolved.

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Антибаран** (anti-ram) | Advance's named skip-and-return rule: don't grind on a stuck item — mark it and move on. Two latency budgets: **10 s** at Part-1 recall checkpoints, **2–3 s** at first-pass encoding. | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §Антибаран |
| **Воронка** (funnel) | Advance's named two-pile latency-sort of a review list (recalled instantly vs. hesitated/missed), re-drilling only the second pile. **Registered as Advance's named instance, not a new mechanism** — the underlying spacing/retrieval-triage mechanic stays owned by [spaced-repetition](./spaced-repetition.md) / [active-recall](./active-recall.md); this row does not redefine it. | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §Воронка |
| **7 типов информации** (seven information types) | Advance's information taxonomy (единичные образы · парочки · цепочки · цифры · графика · связи · комплексные информационные объекты). A finer-grained sibling of Kozarenko's three-way образная/вербальная/точная split; orthogonal to NEDF's functional slot-typing. | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §The seven information types |
| **Проверочный блок / Сводный проверочный блок** | Advance's Part 1 checkpoint shape: a per-block written recall test in both directions, plus two consolidated (сводный) checkpoints and a final all-list sweep. | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §The Part 1 protocol shape |
| **Recall overtakes image** | Advance's relative-ordering automaticity pass-floor: drill until the translation is retrieved *faster* than the mnemonic image/association — typically the 5th–7th repetition. A relative (no-stopwatch) instrument, complementary to (not a replacement for) the wiki's absolute-time floors; level placement, if any, is [skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md)'s call. | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §Recall overtakes image |
| **Договор** (the reading contract) | Advance's fill-in-the-blank self-contract opening the workbook: minutes/day, deadline, self-reward, pre-authorized retry on miss. | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §The reading contract |
| **Advance's 9 Principles / 5 Myths** | Advance's stated language-learning theory: 9 principles (изоляция · специализация · priority of active actions · sequence · high tempo · automaticity · repetition · measurability · wings-or-springboard) and 5 named myths (incl. overrating immersion, overrating passive input). The theory-level basis of the rival account to [Krashen](./krashen-sla-hypotheses.md). | [yagodkin-advance-mnemonics](./yagodkin-advance-mnemonics.md) §The nine principles / §The five myths |
| **Когнитивный домик** (Cognitive House) | Advance's four-storey architectural metaphor for the mind: neurodynamics (rechargeable-battery foundation) → basic mental functions (interlocking gears) → higher mental functions (flowers/fruit) → skills and competencies (flat roof, where the load gets stacked). | [cognitive-house-model](./cognitive-house-model.md) |
| **Skill Isolation Principle (Advance)** | Advance's attention-allocation rule for skill installation: split a skill into sub-skills, train one at 100% attention. **Not** the Von Restorff isolation effect (the pre-existing memory-salience entry above, owned by [serial-position-curve](./serial-position-curve.md)) — same word, unrelated concept, disambiguated in-page on the owner. | [cognitive-house-model](./cognitive-house-model.md) §Skill Isolation Principle |
| **Intensity Principle (Advance)** | Advance's second skill-installation principle — "isolation in time": short maximum-speed sprints (30 s–2 min), stopped at the first sign of fatigue; explicitly contrasted against the load-to-failure model of muscle training. | [cognitive-house-model](./cognitive-house-model.md) §Intensity Principle |
| **Штурм / Storm** | Advance's named single-session assault regime: one massed high-volume push (ramped 100→200→400→700→1,000 words over the standard build-up), culminating in a "day of records". Scheduling layer only — the encoding technique it consumes is owned elsewhere ([substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md)). | [storm-and-siege-protocol](./storm-and-siege-protocol.md) |
| **Осада / Siege** | Advance's named daily-accumulation regime: small, sustainable, ideally-daily steps — накопление's (Storm's) preservation and habit-formation counterpart. | [storm-and-siege-protocol](./storm-and-siege-protocol.md) |
| **Day of records** | Advance's closing-course event: memorize as many information units as possible in one day, with tiered volume bars (300 floor · 600 target · 1,000 stretch · 1,500–2,000 records). Volume bars, not [skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md) levels — deliberately not cited against that ladder. | [storm-and-siege-protocol](./storm-and-siege-protocol.md) §Day-of-records tiers |
| **The Storm ramp** | Advance's pre-storm build-up sequence: 100 → 200 → 400 → 700 → 1,000 words (non-doubling gaps), with a loaded "rest day" between rungs. A session-volume ladder, not a skill-stage ladder — deliberately not cited against [skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md). | [storm-and-siege-protocol](./storm-and-siege-protocol.md) §The Storm ramp |
| **МегаЛоция** (MegaLocus) | Advance's whole-environment re-skin applied per class (e.g. a "winter" setting for AAA irregular verbs): class membership is read off the scene's genre rather than tagged per item. Distinct from Method of Loci's *order* ([memory-palace](./memory-palace.md)) and from per-item marker tagging ([mental-markers-category-importance-order](./mental-markers-category-importance-order.md)). | [vocabulary-word-type-routing](./vocabulary-word-type-routing.md) |

**Not registered (deliberate, 2026-07-16).** Advance's **образный алфавит** (image alphabet) — a fixed image-per-syllable/sound system for a target language — is **not** given its own registry row. It is a sibling *instance* of the same fixed-image-code pattern БЦК instantiates (the pattern is owned by [Major System](./mnemonic-methods-master.md)), confirmed **not** the same table as [БЦК](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) — different school, different alphabet (syllables/sounds vs. digits), no shared table. See [kozarenko-mnemotechnics](./kozarenko-mnemotechnics.md) §Образный алфавит for the full disambiguation.

## Mental-wellbeing layer (Leahy Worry Cure ingest, 2026-08-31)

Added 2026-08-31 from Robert L. Leahy, *The Worry Cure: Seven Steps to Stop Worry from Stopping You* (Harmony/Crown, 2005; 2006 printing). Five new owner pages: leahy-worry-cure (source-summary and routing hub, no glyph), productive-vs-unproductive-worry (glyph 🎛️), worry-as-emotional-avoidance (glyph 🧯), intolerance-of-uncertainty (glyph 🪤), health-anxiety (glyph 🔬). No new Neural OS encoder or acronym-family — Leahy's material enters as domain content routed onto existing frameworks (NEDF cards, METER floors, [skill-progression-stages](./skill-progression-stages.md) drill-ladder axis), the same "source feeding existing owners" pattern as the Kozarenko/Yagodkin ingests above.

**IUS vs IU — deliberate split, do not collapse.** **IU** (Intolerance of Uncertainty) is the *construct* — the affective inability to tolerate not-knowing. **IUS** (Intolerance of Uncertainty Scale) is the *instrument* that measures it, one of five questionnaires in Leahy's worry-profile battery. They get two different owners on purpose: IU is a mechanism a page can teach and drill, IUS is a citation to a specific psychometric tool. Collapsing them would make "the IUS says X" and "IU explains X" indistinguishable, which is exactly the construct/instrument conflation the split exists to prevent.

| Term | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| **Productive worry** | Leahy's Step-One triage verdict: a worry that is plausible, actionable now or very soon, and converts to a concrete step inside 48 hours. Passes all three gates → leaves the worry system as a to-do item. | productive-vs-unproductive-worry |
| **Unproductive worry** | The triage verdict for a worry that fails any of the three productive-worry gates — an unanswerable question, a chain-reaction, a perfect-solution demand, a feeling-as-stop-rule, or a control-everything demand. Routed to worry time, not to problem-solving. | productive-vs-unproductive-worry |
| **Worry time** | The containment protocol for unproductive worry: a fixed daily slot (~30 min, prescribed 2 weeks) in which the worry is written out without challenging or reassuring; off-schedule worries are carded and postponed to the slot. A terminator-by-clock, not by resolution. | productive-vs-unproductive-worry |
| **Intolerance of Uncertainty (IU)** | The affective inability to tolerate not-knowing — the unknown is scored as if it were already the bad outcome. Leahy calls it "the most important element in worry." Domain-local (can be high in one life area, low in another). **Construct**, not the instrument — see IUS below. | intolerance-of-uncertainty |
| **Safety behaviors** | The moves that buy momentary certainty while maintaining the underlying intolerance — reassure · check · collect information · over-prepare · avoid. Each works immediately and therefore reinforces the belief that certainty must be hunted, not tolerated. | intolerance-of-uncertainty |
| **Uncertainty flooding** | Repeated, deliberate exposure to an unknowable sentence ("it's always possible that ___") with every neutralizing move withheld; pass criterion is boredom, not relief or resolution. Leahy's named counter-move for IU. | intolerance-of-uncertainty |
| **Worry as emotional avoidance** | Borkovec's mechanism, via Leahy: worry substitutes an abstract verbal loop for the vivid imagery emotional processing requires, so arousal never rises, never decays, and the fear is never extinguished. The *why* underneath unproductive worry's persistence. | worry-as-emotional-avoidance |
| **Articulate-calm false negative** | The named instrumentation failure this mechanism causes: an operator mid-worry-loop self-reports as calm and rational and passes every emotion-noticing drill, because the loop has suppressed the very signal those drills look for. | worry-as-emotional-avoidance |
| **Channel check** | The instrumentation fix for the articulate-calm false negative: ask "am I verbal-looping right now?" *before* trusting a body scan or state-naming drill. A clean scan taken mid-loop is a null result, not a pass. | worry-as-emotional-avoidance |
| **Thought-action fusion** | Treating the *having* of a thought as equivalent to, or causal of, the corresponding action — "as if the thought and the action are the same." General form; feared object is the operator's own impulse. | health-anxiety |
| **Thought-reality fusion** | The health-anxiety variant of thought-action fusion: the belief that having a thought will make something real happen — feared object is the operator's own body/mortality, treated by checking and reassurance-seeking rather than suppression/avoidance. | health-anxiety |
| **Metacognitive beliefs about worry (Wells's five)** | Beliefs whose object is *the act of worrying itself*, not the feared content: positive beliefs · uncontrollability and danger · cognitive self-consciousness · need to control worry · cognitive confidence. Measured by the Metacognitions Questionnaire (MCQ). Elevation on positive beliefs *and* danger beliefs simultaneously is the normal chronic-worrier pattern, not a contradiction to resolve. | leahy-worry-cure |
| **Worry profile** | Leahy's Ch. 3 intake battery — five questionnaires (PSWQ, WDQ, MCQ, IUS, PBQ) that together target which later techniques a given worrier needs, rather than running the whole program undifferentiated. | leahy-worry-cure |
| **PSWQ — Penn State Worry Questionnaire** | Borkovec et al.'s single-factor worry-severity instrument. Anchors: nonanxious ≈ 30, some problem > 52, chronic worriers > 65. | leahy-worry-cure |
| **WDQ — Worry Domains Questionnaire** | Tallis, Davey & Bond's five-content-domain worry instrument (relationships, lack of confidence, aimless future, work, financial). | leahy-worry-cure |
| **MCQ — Metacognitions Questionnaire** | Wells's 30-item instrument measuring the five metacognitive-belief factors above; no established norms, read the relative profile. | leahy-worry-cure |
| **IUS — Intolerance of Uncertainty Scale** | Freeston/Ladouceur-lineage instrument measuring five uncertainty factors. Anchors: <40 tolerant, >50 problematic, >70 real difficulty, GAD mean 87. **Instrument**, not the construct — see IU above. | leahy-worry-cure |
| **PBQ — Personal Beliefs Questionnaire** | U. Penn Medical School instrument mapping personality-style beliefs onto Oldham's categories; no norms, read which styles are elevated. | leahy-worry-cure |
| **Looming vulnerability** | Riskind's model, via Leahy: felt urgency is a perceptual read of a threat's *rate of closure*, not a fact about the deadline — the "hundred trains" effect of scanning a whole task list at once. Names the failure mode where a sound schedule is abandoned under manufactured urgency and misdiagnosed as a planning defect. | time-management-os |

## U — See (CAST)
1. Graph of registered names → owner pages
2. Edges: "owns", "collides-with", "first-mention-of"

## D — Name (NEDF)
1. Glossary = controlled-vocabulary registry
2. Names live here; definitions on owner pages
3. Drift here causes drift everywhere

## F — Do (SPEAR)
1. New term? Add here first, then use
2. First mention on a page? Link to owner
3. Term collision? Pick a different name

## B — Watch (HEART)
1. Capital-letter acronyms not in registry
2. Parallel definitions on non-owner pages
3. Overloaded letters (T, E, R)

## L — Predict (ORACLE)
1. Skipping glossary → silent collisions
2. Drift accelerates as wiki grows

## R — Act (GRACE)
1. About to coin acronym → check collisions
2. Found drift in audit → fix at owner, re-register
